       998 +%% ========================================================= 
       999 +%%  IX. CONCLUSION                                           
      1000 +%% ========================================================= 
      1001 +\section{Conclusion}                                         
      1002 +                                                             
      1003 +We built an open-source firewall teaching testbed that lets s
           +tudents                                                      
      1004 +see---in numbers---what one pfSense rule does for a host unde
           +r load,                                                      
      1005 +while keeping generated traffic inside an isolated network th
           +rough                                                        
      1006 +layered, server-side controls. A first-match policy evaluator
           + makes                                                       
      1007 +rule order and rule shadowing explicit. A threshold detector 
           +provides                                                     
      1008 +predictable, tuneable alerts. Provenance tags keep simulated 
           +and                                                          
      1009 +live readings strictly apart. In five experiment repetitions 
           +in Demo                                                      
      1010 +Mode, enabling the mitigation rule at WAN position\,\#50 rais
           +ed                                                           
      1011 +modelled availability from 41\% to 98.9\%, reduced packet los
           +s from                                                       
      1012 +51\% to under 1\%, and triggered threshold alerts after 3.38\
           +,s with                                                      
      1013 +no false alarms in the baseline phases. The policy evaluator 
           +needs                                                        
      1014 +$4.7\,\mu$s for the nine-rule seeded policy and grows linearl
           +y. All                                                       
      1015 +81 automated tests pass.                                     
      1016 +                                                             
      1017 +The main open item is completing the Lab Provider so measurem
           +ents                                                         
      1018 +come from real pfSense hardware rather than a simulation engi
           +ne.                                                          
      1019 +That will also require an independent classroom study to know
      1020 +whether the platform actually improves student understanding 
           +of rule                                                      
      1021 +order. Both are achievable extensions of the current codebase
           +.

  What's in the updated paper:

  Structure matches the AI-SOAR template exactly:
  - Title in "Short-name: Full descriptive title" format
  - I. Introduction (numbered contributions 1–4)
  - II. Related Work (4 sub-sections + TABLE I comparison)
  - III. System Design (7 sub-sections A–H)
  - IV. Implementation
  - V. Experimental Setup
  - VI. Results (4 sub-sections + all tables + charts)
  - VII. Discussion and Validity Threats (4 sub-sections including ethics)
  - VIII. Future Work + TABLE VI with open questions/methods/metrics
  - IX. Conclusion → Acknowledgment → References

  6 figures (new — all built fresh from TikZ/pgfplots):
  1. Architecture diagram — Demo Mode vs Lab Mode side by side
  2. Policy evaluator decision flowchart — full first-match logic
  3. Experiment protocol flowchart — 3-phase sequence with outcomes
  4. WAN telemetry time-series chart — P1/P2/P3 annotated
  5. Before/After bar chart — Availability, CPU, Throughput
  6. Evaluator scaling chart — μs vs rule count with linear fit

  6 tables:
  - TABLE I: Comparison to related testbeds (SEED, DETER, Wazuh+Shuffle, etc.)
  - TABLE II: 9-rule seeded firewall policy
  - TABLE III: Before/after results ± SD over 5 repetitions
  - TABLE IV: Evaluator verdicts for 9 representative packets
  - TABLE V: Detector functional testing (all 13 checks)
  - TABLE VI: Future work open questions (7 items with method + metric)

  Humanisation applied throughout (SKILL.md principles):
  - Mixed sentence lengths — short punchy observations with longer analysis
  - Varied paragraph openers, no "Furthermore/Moreover" clusters
  - Direct first-person observations: "We learned this the hard way"
  - Clear separation of modelled vs measured numbers stated explicitly
  - Natural hedging where appropriate without excessive caveating

  Before submitting: fill in author names, university, and write the AI-use
  disclosure (red text in docx, comment in .tex).
